# Verification Report — Supermortgage Origination Build Specification

Six independent verification passes were run against the completed specs on 2026-09-11, none of which took the specs on trust: two re-verified every regulatory timer and gate against its primary source, two adversarially spot-checked the highest-risk citations, and two audited cross-section consistency (timers, notices, tables, cross-references, event names, partner/SM boundary statements, hand-offs, template conformance). Every correction the passes could apply as a local edit was applied to the process files before publication; the few that require a coordinated change are listed under "Corrections required (not applied)" in each pass and carried into the master index as open items.

---

## Timer re-verification — sections §20–§25

## T-A — Timer re-verification, sections §20–§25 (regulatory / Fannie Mae / state timer codes)

**Scorecard:** 154 unique non-`SM_` codes in `build/verify/timer-codes-A.txt` (the "Value-acceptance offer age" pseudo-row is `FNMA_B4_1_4_10_VALUE_ACCEPTANCE_OFFER_4M`) — **146 CONFIRMED · 5 SPEC WRONG (2×S1, 3×S3) · 2 BLUEPRINT WRONG (spec already corrects) · 1 UNRESOLVED** — plus 3 annotation clean-ups (S4) where a "[UNVERIFIED]" tag could now be resolved. All five SPEC WRONG items were corrected in the process files (7 files touched: 20.2, 21.3, 21.4, 21.6, 23.1, 23.4, 24.2 — see "Corrections applied"). Every primary source was fetched 2026-09-11; eCFR "current as of" dates and Selling Guide effective dates are given per row. `build/timer-registry.csv` and `build/sections/` were **not** edited (assembled artifacts) — they must be regenerated from the process files.

Method note: eCFR, consumerfinance.gov, uscode.house.gov, selling-guide.fanniemae.com, singlefamily.fanniemae.com, leg.colorado.gov, malegislature.gov, azleg.gov, le.utah.gov, LII state mirrors and public.law were reachable through the fetch tool; direct `curl` is policy-blocked, so long Official-Interpretation pages could be read only for their first ~third (comment 19(e)(1)(iv)-1 was the last visible §1026.19 comment) — every §1026.19 timer below is therefore verified against the regulation text itself plus the comments that were visible. Colorado's signed act PDF, the DU V.12.1 Sept 25, 2026 release notes/impact memo, and the Fed's Consumer Compliance Handbook (Reg H flood) were fetched in full.

Business-day legend used below: **Reg Z general** = creditor's offices open (§1026.2(a)(6) first sentence; spec unit `business_days_creditor`); **Reg Z specific** = all calendar days except Sundays and 5 U.S.C. 6103(a) holidays (spec unit `business_days_regz_specific`); **Reg X** = entity's offices open (§1024.2(b)); **Reg B** defines none; **Fannie Mae** Guide does not define "business day" for the VVOE/C2 rules.

## T-B — Timer re-verification, sections §26–§31 (regulatory / Fannie Mae / MERS / state timers)

**Verifier run date:** 2026-09-11. Input: `build/verify/timer-codes-B.txt` (181 lines; SM_ policy timers excluded) and `build/timer-registry.csv`. Every code below was located in its process file and re-read against a primary source fetched today (eCFR "up to date as of" pages, uscode.house.gov, selling-guide / servicing-guide / singlefamily.fanniemae.com topics and user guides with their dates, mersinc.org procedure manuals, fincen.gov, finra.org, sifma.org, leg.colorado.gov, cppa.ca.gov, law.lis.virginia.gov, leg.state.fl.us, azleg.gov, codes.ohio.gov, NMLS Resource Center). Where only a mirror was reachable (Texas Constitution and 7 TAC ch. 153 — the official Texas sites are JavaScript-only / moved to an Appian portal) the verdict says so.

### Scorecard

**135 findings rows covering 163 distinct codes (the 181 input lines de-duplicated): CONFIRMED 115 (three of them carry an UNRESOLVED sub-element — the PE–WL API quote window, the 14:00 ET early-close cut-off, and the Texas constitutional text of §50(f)(2)) · SPEC WRONG 14 rows = 13 distinct defects (S1 0 · S2 4 · S3 6 · S4 3; the OFAC blocked-property anchor appears in both 28.4 and 31.3) · BLUEPRINT WRONG 1 (first-payment "62 days", already corrected by the spec) · UNRESOLVED 1 (`FNMA_TSP_PRODUCTION_CERT_GATE`, no public source) · reference-only rows 5.** All 13 defects (including one coverage gap) were corrected in the process files — 32 edits listed under "Corrections applied". No S1 found.

S2 items: (1) `OFAC_501_601_RETENTION_10Y` — wrong anchor for blocked property (must run from unblocking, not transaction date); (2) `FNMA_A3_2_01_COMPLIANCE_SELF_REPORT_60` — spec modelled only Reporting Category 1 (volume threshold) and missed Category 2 (any breach that could warrant repurchase and is not remedied within 60 days → self-report within 60 days); (3) `FNMA_A4_1_01_ANNUAL_ORIGINATION_1B_0131` — origination-liquidity applicability tested annually on the prior calendar year, but A4-1-01 uses "the most recent four-quarter period" (rolling, quarterly); (4) `FNMA_TT96_FIRST_LAR_ACQ_MONTH_BD2` — first-LAR hard deadline set at BD2 17:00 ET of the following month, but the TT96 user guide requires the LAR "in the same month that the loans are acquired" and the IRM's outer limit for prior-period non-removal activity is BD1 20:00 ET.

### Corrections applied
1. `sections/§31/31.1.md:112` — `origination_liquidity_applies` redefined as trailing-four-quarter originations > $1B (excluding reverse, C-to-P to home buyers, lot loans), recomputed each quarter close — A4-1-01 (08/05/2026) "most recent four-quarter period".

### Corrections required (not applied)
1. **Code names now misleading** — `FNMA_TT96_FIRST_LAR_ACQ_MONTH_BD2` (deadline is end of the acquisition month, not BD2) and `FNMA_A4_1_01_ANNUAL_ORIGINATION_1B_0131` (test is quarterly/rolling). Both are referenced from `sections/30/_overview.md`, `sections/30/_closing.md`, `sections/§30/30.4.md:101` and the generated registry; renaming needs a coordinated pass (suggest `FNMA_TT96_FIRST_LAR_ACQ_MONTH_EOM` and `FNMA_A4_1_01_ORIG_LIQUIDITY_APPLIES_Q`). Semantics were fixed in place; only the identifiers remain stale. (S4)
2. **A3-2-01 OFAC-match notice (servicing side)** — "notify Fannie Mae Ethics via email within 24 hours of identifying a match" during periodic servicing screening is outside §26–§31; confirm the servicing spec (project spec-18/-19 or sections 12.x/19.x) carries it. (S3, out of scope here)
3. **`FNMA_B8_8_02_ENOTE_SIGNING_RECORDS_LIFE_PLUS_7Y` after a servicing transfer-out** — "life of the loan plus seven years" is anchored on the loan, not on SM's servicing tenure; the spec's "later of liquidation / transfer-out" is safe only if the signing records are handed to the transferee servicer with the file (31.3/17.x design point, not a local edit). (S4)
4. **HMDA March 1 falling on a weekend** — the CFPB/FFIEC filing practice (next business day) was not verified today; the spec's "no adjustment" reading is conservative and stands. (S4 / U)
5. **PE–Whole Loan early-close cut-off (14:00 ET on SIFMA early-close days)** and the **Loan Pricing API quote-ID window** remain UNRESOLVED — no public source; keep the existing flags in 29.2/29.1.
6. **Texas constitutional text of §50(f)(2)** — the enrolled SJR 60 pages at capitol.texas.gov returned no text today (JavaScript-only); 7 TAC §153.45 (mirror) carries the same one-year, 3-business-day and 12-day rules, so no correction is needed, but the constitutional citation stays "mirror/PARTIALLY VERIFIED" as the spec already marks it.

## C-A — Adversarial citation spot-check, sections §20–§25

Verifier pass run 2026-09-11 against primary sources fetched the same day (eCFR "up to date as of" pages, CFPB Official Interpretations, federalregister.gov, selling-guide.fanniemae.com topics with their "last updated" stamps, singlefamily.fanniemae.com lender letters/matrices/release notes, leg.colorado.gov, statutes.capitol.texas.gov, the LII mirror of 3 NYCRR). Fourteen load-bearing claim families were selected; each family was checked on every number, anchor and quoted phrase the §20–§25 process files depend on, and the worked examples/tests that hang off them were recomputed.

All five defects corrected in the per-process files; 25 edits applied; nothing required re-architecture.

### Corrections required (not applied)

None. Two loose ends are recorded for the owning writers rather than edited: (1) 22.4 line 22's attribution of the "Monthly bank statements must be dated within 45 days … Quarterly … 90 days" sentence to B3-4.2-01 could not be confirmed on today's fetch (the topic page rendered only the manual-underwriting "more than 45 days earlier than the date of the loan application" sentence) — UNRESOLVED, no behavioral impact because rule R3 already applies the 45-day test; (2) 21.6/21.1's Federal Register page number "91 FR 21620" for the Reg B April 2026 final rule was not read on a primary page (the rule's effective date July 21, 2026 and substance were confirmed on consumerfinance.gov).

## Adversarial citation spot-check — sections §26–§31

## Verification report C-B — Adversarial citation spot-check, sections §26–§31

Verifier pass date: **2026-09-11**. Scope: the 14 most load-bearing claims in `sections/O07 … sections/§31` (plus 9 supporting spot-checks that anchor timers in the same files). Every primary source below was fetched today through WebFetch (the egress proxy denies `curl` to selling-guide.fanniemae.com, singlefamily.fanniemae.com, ecfr.gov and statutes.capitol.texas.gov, so long PDFs were read through the fetch tool's extraction — where that extraction was truncated the item is marked UNRESOLVED rather than guessed).

### Scorecard

**23 claims checked (14 primary + 9 supporting): CONFIRMED 17 · SPEC WRONG 5 (S2 ×1, S3 ×1, S4 ×3) · BLUEPRINT WRONG 1 (spec already correct) · UNRESOLVED 3 sub-items (RDC §9.4.1 SLA table; C2-1.1-06 day-count convention; Fannie Mae's own §1026.39 letter instruction) — all three were already flagged as unverified in the specs.** No S1. Eleven edits applied across 9 process files; nothing required re-architecture.

### Corrections applied
1. `sections/§29/29.1.md:36` — replaced 'delivery must occur "on or before the date upon which the commitment expires."' with the verbatim C2-1.2-03 lead-in and conditions ("the acquisition date is on or before the date upon which the commitment expires") plus a note that the timer is satisfied only by `loan.purchased` — because the Guide's condition is the acquisition date, not delivery (C2-1.2-03, 03/03/2021).
2. `sections/§29/29.1.md:14` (Nuances) — "lives in Fannie Mae's PE–WL documentation, not in the Selling Guide topics as served" → "is stated in Selling Guide C2-2-01 ('Whole Loan Good Delivery Amounts') and restated in Fannie Mae's PE–WL documentation" — C2-2-01 states the band (08/07/2018).
3. `sections/§29/29.1.md:24` — added the four C2-2-01 "Whole Loan Good Delivery Amounts" sentences after "The topic contains no delivery-tolerance percentage" — same source.
4. `sections/§29/29.1.md:49` (discrepancy 3) — rewritten to place the tolerance band in C2-2-01 rather than "not in the Selling Guide topics as served" — same source.
5. `sections/§29/29.4.md:95` — `FNMA_B2_1_5_02_LOAN_AGE_6M_PURCHASE_READY` offset "+6 months (calendar_months, same day-of-month)" → "+6 calendar_months minus one day = the last day of the sixth month counting the first-payment month as month one", with the B2-1.5-02 example quoted; parenthetical "the rule lives in B2-1.5-02, not C1-1-01" → "stated with its worked example in B2-1.5-02 and repeated in C1-1-01" — B2-1.5-02 (06/05/2024) example; C1-1-01 (02/05/2025).
6. `sections/§29/29.4.md:119` (R1) — "Purchase Ready no later than Thu Jul 1, 2027" → "Wed Jun 30, 2027" (with the reason); "224 days" → "223 days" — same source.
7. `sections/§29/29.4.md:191` (29.4-T4) — loan-age due date "Thu Jul 1, 2027" → "Wed Jun 30, 2027 (a Purchase Ready date of Thu Jul 1, 2027 breaches it)" — same source.
8. `sections/29/_closing.md:11` (IT-6) — loan-age clock "Thu Jul 1, 2027" → "Wed Jun 30, 2027" with the reason — same source.
9. `sections/§29/29.1.md:107` — reference row offset aligned to 29.4's corrected definition — same source.
10. `sections/§29/29.3.md:47` (discrepancy 3) — added the ULDD FAQ Q6 nuance that Loan Delivery labels Sort ID 642 "Wire Nickname" and 650.1 "Warehouse Lender", so the label does not imply SID 398.3 — ULDD FAQ Q6; Wire Instruction Reference Identifier guide (5.14.25) "standard MBS Pools only".

(Items 5–9 are one correction applied across its dependent worked example, test and cross-references.)

### Corrections required (not applied)
1. **Research digest `research/00b-orig-integration-landscape.md` lines 105, 109 and 285** treat "SID 398.3 Wire Instruction Reference Identifier (Wire Nickname)" as a whole-loan delivery identifier that is "conditionally required in the ULDD XML import file". Fannie Mae's guide (5.14.25) says the field "is for standard MBS Pools only"; whole-loan cash wires are Form 482 payee codes carried in Sort ID 642 (+ 650.1 under a bailee letter). The digest is a research input, not a process file, so it was not edited; the orchestrator should annotate it so no downstream section re-imports the error (29.3/29.4 already have the correct rule).
2. **Assembled copies and registry under `build/`** (`build/sections/O10-secondary-and-delivery.md`, `build/timer-registry.csv` line 475–491 region, `build/consistency-prepass.md`/`.json`) still carry the old "+6 months (calendar_months, same day-of-month)" offset and the "Jul 1, 2027" fixture date for `FNMA_B2_1_5_02_LOAN_AGE_6M_PURCHASE_READY`; regenerate from the per-process files.
3. **Timer name `FNMA_B2_1_5_FIRST_PAYMENT_2M`** (26.3; referenced by 25.3, 26.2, 29.4 and the registry) carries a B2-1.5 anchor although the rule is C2-2-01 (the 26.3 row already says "source C2-2-01"). Renaming touches five files and the registry — cosmetic (S4), recommended for a naming pass, not applied.
4. **RDC v15.0 §9.4.1 "Service Level Requirement for Certifications"** could not be read (PDF extraction truncated); the spec's use of the Job Aids v4.0 daily SLA ("CASH 4:00 PM EST") remains the operative figure and is already labelled PARTIALLY VERIFIED. Obtain the RDC PDF directly to close it.

## Cross-section consistency audit — timers, notices, tables, cross-references

## A-1 — Cross-section consistency audit, part 1: timers, notices, tables, cross-references

**Verifier:** independent consistency pass, 2026-09-11. Inputs: `build/consistency-prepass.md/.json` (machine pre-pass), `research/writer-brief.md` ownership map, every `sections/O0N/ON.M.md` timer table and notice list (re-parsed with a stricter reference detector), the servicing specs in the project (`claude/spec-01`, `-03`, `-05`, `-07`, `-11`, `-16`, `-19`) for the servicing-owned codes, and four primary sources fetched today where a canonical definition had to be settled against the rule.

**Scorecard:** 41 machine-flagged timer codes + 30 multi-process notice codes + 8 additional same-obligation/duplicate-name pairs found by hand + 2 tables/classes declared new twice + 3 dangling code/event references reviewed → **32 CONFIRMED (consistent references, no change)**, **36 SPEC WRONG / consistency defects (all corrected in place)**, **1 UNRESOLVED (servicing-side anchor, not applied)**, **0 BLUEPRINT WRONG**. By severity of the defects: S1 0 · S2 6 · S3 22 · S4 8. Dangling origination process references (O13.x, non-existent ON.M): none found. Out-of-range servicing process references (N.M beyond the 114-process index): none found.

Method note: the machine pre-pass counts a row as a "definition" unless its first cell starts with "(reference"/"(owned by"/"(see"; rows written as "`CODE` (22.3 — reference)", "(7.4 owns)", "(21.6; referenced)" or "Composed (owned elsewhere…)" were therefore flagged as conflicting definitions. Every one of the 41 flagged codes was read in context; 27 of them were genuine references with no conflicting anchor/offset/kind and are listed as CONFIRMED below (only where a reference row restated the rule *differently* from the owner was it edited).

### Corrections required (not applied)
1. **Post-purchase-adjustment data model split (27.2 ↔ 29.4) — S3.** 27.2 declares `ppa_requests` (new; kinds funds_transfer_error/data_correction; events `ppa.requested`/`ppa.resolved`) and 29.4 declares `post_purchase_adjustments` (new; initiated_by seller/fnma_lqc; LSDU attributes; events `post_purchase_adjustment.submitted/processed`). Two tables and two event families for one Fannie Mae PPA. The timer codes are now single-owner (items 4–5), but merging the tables (recommend: keep 29.4's `post_purchase_adjustments` as the record of the LSDU/PPA request and have 27.2 write `kind=funds_transfer_error` rows into it, retiring `ppa_requests`) is a data-model change in two processes, not a local edit.
2. **Servicing 1.1 `LL_2026_05_ESCROW_SETUP_ACQUIRED_BD1` anchor — S3 (servicing spec).** Generalize the anchor to "the later of `boarded_at` and Fannie Mae acquisition (`fnma_established_at`/purchase date)" so that 30.1's origination instantiation (anchor_event `loan.purchased`) is an instance of the definition rather than a documented deviation; the offset (next `business_days_fannie_et` 03:00 ET) is consistent and stays [PARTIALLY VERIFIED].
3. **Servicing 7.4 `TCPA_REVOCATION_HONOR_10BD` vs 11.1 `TCPA_64_1200_A10_REVOCATION_HONOR_10BD` — S3 (servicing spec).** Same 47 CFR 64.1200(a)(10) rule under two codes with two units (`business_days_servicer` vs `business_days_federal`); origination references 11.1's code only. Retire one and settle the unit (the rule says "ten business days" without a definition; the federal calendar is the conservative reading).
4. **Unescaped `|` inside code spans in timer rows (item 37) — S4.** Escape as `\|` in an assembly-time pass so Markdown renderers and the machine registry read seven cells.

### Sources fetched (2026-09-11)
- 12 CFR 1003.5 — https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-12/chapter-X/part-1003/section-1003.5 (current as of 9/08/2026): (a)(1)(i) annual submission by March 1 and "retain a copy … for at least three years"; (a)(1)(ii) quarterly within 60 calendar days; (a)(1)(iii) weekend rule.
- 31 CFR 1029.320 — https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-31/subtitle-B/chapter-X/part-1029/subpart-C/section-1029.320 (current as of Sept 1, 2026): paragraph (c) Retention of records — SAR copy and supporting documentation "for a period of five years from the date of filing the SAR".
- 31 CFR 501.601 — https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-31/subtitle-B/chapter-V/part-501/subpart-C/section-501.601 (current as of 9/08/2026): records "for at least 10 years after the date of such transaction"; blocked property "for the period of time that such property is blocked and for at least 10 years after the date such property is unblocked".
- Fannie Mae Selling Guide A3-4-03, Preventing, Detecting, and Reporting Mortgage Fraud — https://selling-guide.fanniemae.com/sel/a3-4-03/preventing-detecting-and-reporting-mortgage-fraud (last updated Dec 10, 2025): "notify Fannie Mae within 30 days" via the Loan Quality Connect self-report when "a reasonable basis exists to conclude any misrepresentation or fraud occurred in connection with the origination, sale, or servicing of the loan".
- Project docs (servicing build specification, read via project search 2026-09-11): `claude/spec-01-boarding-transfer-in.md` (1.1 `LL_2026_05_ESCROW_SETUP_ACQUIRED_BD1`, `MERS_PROC_REGISTER_UNREGISTERED_7`), `claude/spec-03-escrow-administration.md` (3.1 `REGX_1024_17G_INITIAL_STMT_45`; 3.2/3.3 annual-analysis timers; 3.4 cushion/pre-accrual gates; 3.5 `REGX_1024_34B_PAYOFF_ESCROW_REFUND_20BD`; 3.8 `FNMA_B101_MI_MONTHLY_ESCROW_GATE`, `REGZ_1026_35B3_HPML_ESCROW_5Y_GATE`), `claude/spec-05-investor-reporting-remittance.md` (5.1 `FNMA_LL202605_EVENT_NEXTBD_0300`, `FNMA_IRM_LAR_IRED_CD22_2000`, `FNMA_IRM_BULK_CUTOFF_BD2_1500`, `FNMA_IRM_PERIOD_CLOSE_BD2_1700`), `claude/spec-07-compliance-notices-disclosures.md` (7.4 `ESIGN_7001C_CONSENT_GATE`, `TCPA_REVOCATION_HONOR_10BD`), `claude/spec-11-early-intervention-collections.md` (11.1 `TCPA_64_1200_A10_REVOCATION_HONOR_10BD`), `claude/spec-16-payoff-lien-release.md` (16.1 `REGZ_1026_36C3_PAYOFF_STMT_7BD` mirror), `claude/00-MASTER-INDEX.md` (process counts per section used for the servicing-reference range check), `claude/04-verification-report.md` (prior servicing-side findings A4/A11 on the same codes).
- Origination inputs: `research/writer-brief.md` (ownership map), `research/verify-brief.md`, `build/consistency-prepass.md/.json`, `tools/consistency_prepass.py` (re-run into scratch after the edits: no new multi-owner codes; residual flags are reference rows the script's regex does not recognize).

## Cross-section consistency audit — events, boundaries, hand-offs, template conformance

## A-2 — Cross-section consistency audit, part 2: events, boundary contradictions, hand-offs, template conformance

**Verifier:** independent consistency pass · **Date:** 2026-09-11 · **Corpus:** `sections/O01…§31` (51 process files, 12 overviews, 12 closings; 475,837 words) · **Inputs:** `build/consistency-prepass.md`, `research/architecture-baseline-origination.md` §3/§12, `research/writer-brief.md` (ownership map), `research/spec-template.md`, and the subservicing spec's verified event inventory (project doc `claude/04-verification-report.md`, Appendix B — 987 servicing event names) for every servicing-side name an origination process listens for.

**Method.** (1) Machine pass (`scratchpad/events.py`, a stricter successor to `tools/consistency_prepass.py`): every timer-table `Trigger event` / `Satisfied by` cell was parsed and each event checked against the "events emitted" declarations of every process's Data model and Outputs subsections; 991 → 970 event names after the edits. (2) Manual read of the twelve section overviews and the LoR/SM rows and boundary rules of 26.2, 26.3, 26.4, 27.1, 27.2, 29.3, 29.4, 30.1, 30.4, 31.1 against the nine boundary questions in the assignment. (3) Pairwise read of the eight hand-off pairs. (4) Template conformance script (`scratchpad/conformance.py`) over all 51 files. Nothing was taken on trust: every renamed event was checked against the owning process's declared list, and every worked number I changed was recomputed (Appendix J iteration for the APR; cents arithmetic for prepaid interest and the funding ledger).

**Scorecard:** 64 findings — **42 SPEC WRONG, corrected in place (57 numbered edits across 45 files)** · **14 CONFIRMED** (boundary items and hand-offs that agree across sections) · **6 SPEC WRONG — recorded, not applied** (table merge, coordinated fixture re-derivation, timer-code split, cosmetic table rows) · **2 UNRESOLVED** (member-only MERS eRegistry semantics; the 1.1 timer variant is A-1's). Severity of the applied corrections: S1 0 · S2 20 · S3 17 · S4 5. Template conformance: **0 gaps** (all 51 process files carry every required heading in order; 11–16 tests each, numbered `ON.M-Tk` with no gaps or duplicates and no dangling cross-references; 8–34 sources each; 5–11 open questions each; every section has `_overview.md` and `_closing.md` with the required headings).

---

### 1. Findings table

Verdict key: **SPEC WRONG** = two sections disagree in a way that builds a dead code path, a wrong figure or an ambiguous owner; the "primary source" column names the section that owns the decision (per the assignment's ownership rule and `research/writer-brief.md`) and, for servicing-side names, the servicing spec's declared event. **CONFIRMED** = the sections agree. **UNRESOLVED** = the deciding source is login-gated.

### 3. Corrections required (not applied)
1. **`post_purchase_adjustments` (29.4:77) and `ppa_requests` (27.2:73) are one entity** (`kind ∈ {funds_transfer_error, data_correction}` in 27.2 already spans 29.4's seller-initiated LSDU corrections; 29.4 adds `repricing_eligible`/18-month fields). Merge into one table owned by 27.2 (request lifecycle) with 29.4's LSDU/repricing columns; 29.4 R8 and T11 then read that table. Multi-file schema change — not a local edit. (S3)
2. **Fixture escrow deposit drift.** 26.3:101/:103/:119/:169, 27.1:134/:182, 27.2:117, 26/_closing:12, 27/_closing:24 carry an "illustrative" initial escrow deposit of $1,665.00 (net wire $557,249.57; partner contribution $8,449.57; ledger `partner_haircut_reserve` 844,957 / `sm_funding_cash` 55,724,957 / `escrow_initial_deposit` 166,500), while 30.3 computes the fixture deposit as **$2,062.50** (206,250) and 30.1/30.2/30.4 board that figure; 25.4's examples use a third set (taxes $4,800 → deposit $1,875.00; 30.4-Q6 already flags it). Re-base 26.3/27.x on $2,062.50 (net wire $556,852.07; contribution $8,052.07; `partner_haircut_reserve` 805,207; `sm_funding_cash` 55,685,207; partner mirror `escrow_initial_deposit` 206,250 — the sets still balance at 56,070,000) and 25.4's examples on 30.3's lines. Nine coordinated numeric edits across four sections — recorded rather than applied so the §26/§27 owners re-derive their examples once. (S3)
3. **`LL_2026_05_ESCROW_SETUP_ACQUIRED_BD1` reuse with a different trigger/anchor** (30.1:102 vs servicing 1.1). Either give the origination instantiation its own code (e.g., `LL_2026_05_ESCROW_SETUP_ORIG_PURCHASE_BD1`, defined in 30.1 as a variant of 1.1's rule) or parameterize 1.1's definition by `anchor_event`. A-1 has annotated the row; the code split is theirs to decide. (S3)
4. **Column-valued trigger cells** (finding E43: ten timers in 31.1, 31.2, 28.2, 30.4, 23.3, 23.1, 21.6, 30.1 whose "Trigger event" is a `table.column` or a config flag). Either declare a scheduled-evaluation trigger (`timer.scheduled_evaluation{table, column}`) in the baseline or rename each to a past-tense event the owning process emits. Cosmetic; left for the Timer Engine design. (S4)
5. **`custody_records` holder enums overlap**: 26.2:67 adds `note_location ∈ {settlement_agent, courier, warehouse_custodian, document_custodian, released_form_2009}` and 26.4:68 adds `holder_role ∈ {…, fcc_bailee, sm_designated_custodian, fnma_custodian, fnma_evault, released}` on the same shared table. Keep 26.4's `holder_role` (it encodes the 26.4-Q1 decision) and make 26.2 seed it; drop `warehouse_custodian`. (S4)
6. **Reference-only timer rows with a non-standard cell count** (`29.3:103` "Composed (owned elsewhere…)", `29.4:115` "Referenced (owned elsewhere)", `31.1:145` "Referenced (never redefined)") — unparseable by any generator that reads the timer tables (the servicing audit's C7 pattern). Move each sentence below its table. (S4)
7. **eRegistry field semantics** (finding B12) remain [PARTIALLY VERIFIED] until the MERS eRegistry Procedures (Rel. 16.25, member-only) are read: confirm that a subservicer's Org ID is what Fannie Mae expects in "Master Servicer" (C1-2-04's "(or sub-servicer, if applicable)") and how "Servicing Agent" relates to it. Owner: 30.1 (post-purchase) with 26.2 (registration). (UNRESOLVED)
